# Color&Noise — Chicago 2026 events

96 events. Generated from `research/chicago-summer-2026-events.md` by `scripts/build-event-files.js` — edit the dossier, not this file, then re-run.

Fields: **name · date · neighborhood · summary · category · hashtags · handles · articles · posts**.
Summaries are machine-derived first drafts. Rewrite the ones you build articles on.

**Eyewitness posts:** 3053 across 80 events, parsed from `event scrape/`.
Each post shows platform, handle, date, link and the opening line of its write-up. The **full write-up for every post** lives in `research/posts/D<n>.md` — linked from each event. Complete data including every full description is in `research/events.json`.

Post timestamps were cross-checked against the dossier dates: every event's posts cluster inside its date window (allowing for people posting a day or two late). The only outlier is D62 Renegade Craft, whose posts are from its September edition rather than the May one — it runs twice.

---

## D14 · West Fest Chicago

- **Date:** Jul 17 – 19, 2026
- **Neighborhood:** West Town  ·  *Chicago Ave between Wood and Damen (1800 W. Chicago Ave), West Town*
- **Category:** `nightlife`
- **Summary:** Chicago Ave's house music street festival — 20+ hours of house music, programmed by Bass by the Pound and Empty Bottle, with a bill including DJ Sneak, Derrick Carter, DJ Heather, Jevon Jackson, Tyree Cooper, Diz, Czboogie, Striz and T Mixwell. Plus Kids Fest and Pet Fest.

## D15 · Bronzeville Smooth Jazz Festival

## D37 · Chicago Pride Parade

- **Date:** Sun, Jun 28, 2026
- **Neighborhood:** Lakeview  ·  *From Sheridan & Broadway (3900 N. Broadway) south on Broadway → Halsted → east on Belmont → south on Broadway → east on Diversey to Cannon Drive. Two-mile route.*
- **Category:** `nightlife`
- **Summary:** 55th annual. Theme: "Free to Be Proud" — framed by organizers as honoring "the trailblazers who paved the way to ensuring every person can live openly, authentically, and with dignity." New step-off time of 11am (earlier than historical practice — that change was itself news).

## D44 · El Grito Chicago + 26th Street Mexican Independence Day Parade

- **Date:** El Grito: Sept 12 – 13, 2026
- **Neighborhood:** Grant Park  ·  *Butler Field, Grant Park (350 E. Monroe)*
- **Category:** `music`
- **Summary:** The headline fact: El Grito returned in 2026 after being cancelled in 2025. Organizers paused the 2025 edition "out of care for community safety amid a difficult political climate" — specifically security concerns stemming from federal immigration enforcement operations in Chicago.

## D49 · Juneteenth

- **Date:** DuSable Museum celebration: Jun 19, 2026
- **Neighborhood:** Bronzeville  ·  *470 E. 56th Place*
- **Category:** `music`
- **Summary:** Two Juneteenths, back to back, a mile apart: an institutional one at the DuSable and a neighborhood one on King Drive. The comparison is the article. (b) Juneteenth in its fifth year as a federal holiday — has the commemoration changed, and who is programming it now? (c) Bronzeville tying Juneteenth to Father's Day.

## D79 · 3rd Fridays Open Studios

- **Date:** Third Friday of every month, 7–10pm, year-round
- **Neighborhood:** Bridgeport  ·  *Bridgeport Art Center, 1200 W. 35th St and Zhou B Art Center, 1029 W. 35th St, Bridgeport*
- **Category:** `art`
- **Summary:** Two large art centers on the same street, both free on third Fridays. Bridgeport Art Center holds three curated galleries, artist studios, the Fashion Design Center, the Chicago Ceramic Center, the Skyline Loft and a Sculpture Garden Gallery — you tour studios and meet the artists working in them.
